# Skills · C · part 288

840 entries, council to counter-narrative. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **council**: Put one hard business decision to a council of five AI personas (Alex Hormozi on offer and money, Elon Musk on first principles, Warren Buffett on cash and risk, Mark Zuckerberg on distribution, MrBeast on attention). In one group chat they give opening takes, argue back and forth, crossfire and vote on an option, and you get a scored verdict with the one thing to do this week, plus a page that replays the whole table. Use for "/council", "ask the council", "what would Hormozi / Buffett / Musk say", "should I spend X on A or B". ([galakurpi/yekar-editor-public](https://github.com/galakurpi/yekar-editor-public/tree/HEAD/artifacts/demos/council/skill/council) · 2 copies)

Not for code review, implementation planning, or architecture design. - **counseling-companion**: 心理支持对话伙伴。当用户表达情绪困扰、想倾诉、寻求心理支持时使用——包括但不限于：焦虑、抑郁情绪、压力大、失眠、emo、破防、心累、和家人/伴侣/朋友/同事发生冲突、失恋、孤独、自我怀疑、职场内耗、考试或工作压力、亲人或宠物离世后的哀伤、反复查不出问题的躯体不适（胸闷、胃疼、心慌）等。也用于用户对改变自身习惯矛盾纠结时（想戒烟、戒酒、减肥、运动、不再熬夜或拖延，但「知道该做就是做不到」），以及原生家庭议题（父母控制、「NPD 父母」、重男轻女）、被恋人或领导贬低操控（「被 PUA」）、性相关的羞耻或创伤经历。用户不需要明说「心理咨询」，只要在倾诉情绪或困扰，就应使用本 skill 以共情倾听的方式回应，而不是直接给建议清单。用户提到自伤、自杀念头时必须使用本 skill 的危机协议。注意：用户只是带着情绪词提出实务任务时（「压力大，帮我写个请假邮件」「帮我查心理咨询行业资料」），做任务即可，不按本 skill 展开咨询对话。 ([Kdd233/counseling-companion](https://github.com/Kdd233/counseling-companion/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))

- **counsels-review**: Dispatch one or more counsel personas to review an existing decision, policy, practice, or draft memo. Default panel for parallel review — Ruth Bader Ginsburg (constitutional / civil-rights lens), Lawrence Lessig (regulability / digital-context lens), John Rawls (justice-as-fairness lens). Override with --personas.
